## Reviewer 1 审稿意见（方法严谨性 + 实验设计）

### 总体评分

**Overall**: 2.5/5（Resubmit next cycle）
**一句话总结**: 研究了重要的实际问题（agent 停滞检测），实验设计有广度，但全部评估基于 silver labels 且 gold set 验证 pending，主表比较存在 evaluation scope 不一致，核心 claims 可信度不足。

### 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）

| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |
|------|------|----------|
| Soundness | 2.5/5 | 无 gold-standard 验证；主表 evaluation scope 不一致；circularity 缓解但未解决 |
| Excitement | 3.5/5 | 问题重要且实用（<5ms no GPU）；label model 负面发现有价值；方法论创新有限 |
| Overall | 2.5/5 | 需完成 gold set 验证和修复 evaluation scope 问题后重投 |
| Reproducibility | 3/5 | 训练细节分散在 appendix caption 中；LF 阈值未给出；代码 review 期间不可用 |
| Confidence | 4/5 | 仔细阅读全文，熟悉弱监督和 agent 评估领域 |

---

### Must-Fix (3 items)

**N1** [critical] Gold set 验证 pending——全部 claims 缺乏 ground-truth 支撑
- 位置：§5.8, Limitations
- 论文承认 650-window gold set "human annotation is pending"。所有报告数字均为 silver label 上的评估。Cross-agent transfer 和 feature overlap ablation 被作为 non-circular 证据，但两者仍以 silver labels 为 ground truth。论文自身的 label version bias 事件（§5.6：OH stag rate 从 6.56%→21.4%）恰恰证明 silver labels 可能严重偏离事实。
- 一篇以检测方法论为核心贡献的论文，必须证明其方法确实检测到了目标现象。

**N2** [critical] Table 2 主比较存在 evaluation scope 不一致
- 位置：Table 2 及 footnotes a/b
- 36.9pp 优势比较的是 structural GB 在全部 OH windows (N=150,652) vs probe 在 15% test split (N=6,122)。Conf≥2 列同样是不同范围（43,376 vs 6,122）。论文未报告 structural GB 在同一 6,122-window test split 上的 F1。Headline number 可能被 scope 差异 inflate。

**N3** [critical] Circularity 缓解论证有逻辑漏洞
- 位置：§6.1, §6.2
- Anti-circularity 论证隐含假设 LFs 在 OH 上也是正确的。如果 LFs 在 OH 上系统性标错，cross-agent F1 高只说明成功"迁移"了 LF 偏差。Feature overlap ablation 中 11-feature vs 30-feature 仅差 1.0pp，在 GB cross-agent std=0.0015 的量级上可能不具统计显著性。

---

### Should-Fix (4 items)

**W1** LLM-as-judge 评估不完整
- §3.3.3 footnote 称 Appendix A.6 含 CoT 结果，但 Table 8 仅有 zero-shot 和 5-shot，无 CoT。GPT-5.5 的 22.4% parse failure 导致 F1 仅在 77.6% 样本上计算。

**W2** 主要比较缺乏统计显著性检验
- 36.9pp 差异无 CI 或 significance test。Multi-seed stability 报告 std 但无 formal test。论文在 LF dynamics 和 cmd_rep ablation 中用了 Kruskal-Wallis/McNemar，但最关键的主表比较中缺失。

**W3** Label model 实验公平性存疑
- 5 个 label model 失败但未报告超参搜索范围。Snorkel MeTaL -63.6pp collapse 如此极端需排查 bug。HMM 初始化/收敛诊断未报告。

**W4** $\lambda_3$ inference-time 不可用性讨论不充分
- 移除 $\lambda_3$ 后 F1 降 7.7pp（§6.3），但 Table 2 的 headline numbers 基于 3-LF（含 outcome）。Production-deployable 版本（2-LF）的性能被高估。

---

### 主要优势

1. 问题重要且实用（<5ms/window, no GPU）
2. 诚实的 circularity 分析——论文主动反复讨论此问题
3. 有价值的 negative finding（5 label models 全败 + root cause）
4. 实验广度（encoder/architecture/window/data scaling/instance isolation ablations）
5. LF temporal dynamics cross-agent reversal 是 novel observation
6. Label version bias 发现对 weak supervision on agent data 有启发

---

### Questions to Authors

1. Gold set annotation 预计何时完成？有无 pilot IAA 数据？
2. 能否报告 structural GB 在 probe 的同一 6,122 test split 上的 F1？
3. Label model 超参搜索范围？MeTaL collapse 是否排查了 bug？
4. 2-LF deployable 配置下 Table 2 主要 numbers？
5. 0.931 vs 0.906 的 cross-split variance 完整分布？

### Missing References

ProcessBench (Zheng et al., 2024), AgentBench (Liu et al., 2023), WebArena (Zhou et al., 2023)

**N1** [critical] 正文严重超过 8 页限制

- 具体位置：main.pdf，Pages 1-12
- 问题：ACL long paper 正文限制 8 页（不含 references / limitations / appendix）。通过 pdftotext 分析，Conclusion 段落出现在 page 12，Limitations 从 page 12 开始，References 从 page 13 开始。即正文（title→conclusion）约 12 页，**超出限制约 4 页**。这是 desk reject 级别的格式违规。
- 为什么影响接收：ARR 对页数限制执行严格，超页论文通常不进入实质审查。
- 建议修改方向：(1) Introduction 中 Finding 1-3 和 4 个 contributions 的详细数字移至实验节，intro 只给定性描述和核心数字；(2) Related Work 从 7 段压缩至 4-5 段（合并 Process Mining + Cycle Detection、合并 Noisy Labels + Label Leakage）；(3) §6.1 Circular Evaluation 和 §6.2 Feature Overlap 合并精简——当前三处（intro、§5 caveat、§6.1-6.2）反复论述 circularity，冗余严重；(4) 将 Instance Isolation (Table 7) 和部分 Error Analysis 移至 appendix。

**N2** [critical] Gold set 验证缺失，所有 claim 基于 silver labels

- 具体位置：§5.8 Gold Set Validation，全文所有实验
- 问题：论文多次声称 "cross-coding-agent transfer and feature overlap ablation provide non-circular evidence"，但实际上：(a) cross-agent transfer 仍然是 silver label vs silver label（OH 的 silver labels 由同套 LF 生成）；(b) feature overlap ablation 是在同一 silver label 集上做 feature subset 比较，不能证明 features 检测到了"真正的" stagnation。650-window gold set 已设计完毕但标注 pending——论文在没有任何 ground truth 验证的情况下提交审稿，核心结论的可靠性无法确认。
- 为什么影响接收：EMNLP 对 soundness 要求高。没有 gold set 或至少部分 gold set 验证（即便 100 个 window），论文的 claim 停留在 "我们能很好地重建自己的 labeling function 输出" 层面，无法证明检测到了真正的 stagnation。
- 建议修改方向：至少完成部分 gold set 标注（e.g., 100-200 windows from conf=1 和 conf=3 各半），报告 silver label 与 gold label 的一致率和 Cohen's κ，并在 gold set 上重跑核心实验（structural vs probe vs LLM-judge）。

**N3** [critical] 关键数字比较不公平，abstract/intro 中 overclaiming

- 具体位置：Abstract line 9；Introduction §1 Finding 1 (line 29)；Table 2 (§5.2)
- 问题：
  (a) **"outperform the neural probe by 36.9pp"**：Structural GB 0.967 是在全部 150,652 OH windows 上的 zero-shot transfer；Probe 0.598 是在 6,122 test-split windows 上（footnote a）。评估集不同，直接相减为 36.9pp 不严谨。且 structural features 天然 domain-agnostic（计算重复率/文件操作数不依赖 agent），而 probe 需要 adaptation by design——这不是公平的 "同一设置下" 对比。
  (b) **"the best LLM judge by 21.6pp"**：Structural 0.967 是 OH cross-agent；LLM 0.751 是 SWE-Agent in-domain 5-shot（Appendix A.6）。两个数字来自不同 agent、不同评估集、不同 label 分布。论文辩称 "comparing the LLM's easier in-domain setting against the structural model's harder cross-coding-agent regime"，但 cross-agent structural 评估的 silver labels 同样由 structural-aligned LFs 生成，所以并非无条件地 "harder"。
  (c) Abstract 和 Introduction 将这两个数字作为核心卖点，但 caveats 分散在 footnotes、§6.1、§6.2 中，读者在 abstract/intro 层面获得的印象是不准确的。
- 建议修改方向：(1) 统一评估 scope——在 OH conf≥2 上对比所有方法（包括 LLM-judge 如有预算），或至少对比 structural 和 probe 在相同 test split 上的数字；(2) Abstract 中的比较应限定为同一评估设置，不跨 agent/scope 直接算差值；(3) LLM-judge 比较如果跨 agent，需明确说明并标注为 lower bound 而非直接比较。

---

## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）

**W1** [warning] 方法论新颖性有限——已有工具的新域应用

- 具体位置：§3 Method；§2 Related Work ¶7 (Cycle and Anomaly Detection, line 63)
- 问题：论文核心发现"结构特征在零数据设定下优于神经方法"在 anomaly detection 文献中已知——论文自己在 §2 line 63 承认 "hand-crafted features often outperform learned representations on narrow detection tasks... Our finding parallels this pattern." WS pipeline（3 LFs + MV）是标准 Snorkel/data programming 应用。GRU probe 是常规序列分类器。LLM-as-judge 是标准 baseline。方法论层面没有新的技术贡献。
- 新颖性主要来自：(a) agent trajectory 作为新域；(b) 跨 agent 迁移的实证发现；(c) label model 负面发现。这些更接近 empirical study / application paper 而非方法论论文。对 EMNLP main conference 来说，excitement 可能不够——Findings track 可能更合适。
- 建议：(1) 更诚实地定位为 empirical study，弱化方法论贡献的措辞；(2) 讨论为什么 agent trajectory 域的特殊性使得这些 known patterns 值得重新验证（e.g., semantic stagnation 的独特性）；(3) 如果可能，增加真正新颖的方法贡献（如 agent-aware LF、自适应窗口大小）。

**W2** [warning] 3 LF 限制使 Finding 3（label model 无用）的意义受限

- 具体位置：§5.7 Temporal Label Models；§6.3 Why No Label Model Improves
- 问题：论文测试 5 个 label model 均不优于 MV，将此定位为重要贡献（Contribution 3）。但论文自己解释：root cause 是 "three binary LFs produce at most 2^3=8 voting patterns; conf≥2 windows dominate"——即 3 个 LF 太少，conflict 不够，任何 aggregation 都无用。这是 **design choice 导致的预期结果**，不是关于 label model 本身的 fundamental insight。
- 如果扩展到 10+ LFs 会怎样？论文承认 "remains an open question"。一个关键的 open question 不应该是你自己可以（至少部分）回答但选择不回答的。
- 建议：(1) 至少尝试添加 2-3 个额外 LF（e.g., output length trend、error message repetition、action type entropy）测试 5-6 LF 下 label model 是否有改善；(2) 将 Finding 3 的表述限定为 "in the 3-LF, low-conflict regime"，而非一般性结论。

**W3** [warning] Introduction 过于密集，Finding 1 长达 8 行包含 6+ 个数字

- 具体位置：§1 Introduction, lines 28-35
- 问题：Finding 1 在 Introduction 中占 8 行 TeX，包含：0.995, 0.967, 0.598, 36.9pp, 0.976, 0.967, 0.977, 1.0pp, 0.566 等数字。读者在还没看到实验设置的情况下被大量数字轰炸，难以理解这些数字的意义和 caveats。Introduction 应概述发现的定性含义，数字属于实验节。
- 建议：Finding 1 在 intro 中简化为 3-4 行定性描述 + 1-2 个关键数字（如 "structural features achieve 0.967 F1 in cross-agent transfer, outperforming neural approaches that near chance without adaptation"），详细数字和 caveats 放到 §5。

**W4** [warning] Circularity 讨论冗余，越辩越心虚

- 具体位置：§1 line 31, 33-34；§5 "Circular evaluation caveat" (lines 11-14)；§6.1 Circular Evaluation and Its Implications；§6.2 Feature Overlap Ablation (lines 50-55)；§7 Conclusion line 22
- 问题：论文在至少 5 处讨论 circularity/feature overlap 问题：Introduction（两处）、§5 caveat、§6.1、§6.2、Conclusion。每一处都用不同的措辞和角度重复同一个论点（"cross-agent transfer breaks circularity"）。这种过度防御的写法反而引起 reviewer 更多怀疑——如果结论真的 robust，不需要反复辩护。
- 建议：(1) 在 §5 caveat 中简洁说明一次（3-4 句话），其他位置仅 cross-reference；(2) §6.1 和 §6.2 合并为一个精简的 ablation 分析；(3) Introduction 和 Conclusion 中各用一句话提及即可。

**W5** [warning] Title 可能误导

- 具体位置：Title
- 问题：Title "Are Stagnation Signals Structural?" 暗示论文会回答 yes 或 no。实际答案是 "yes in zero-data transfer, no with fine-tuning"——即结构信号在零数据设置下足够，但 fine-tuned probe 可以超越。这个 nuanced 的答案无法用 yes/no 概括。标题设定了一个读者期望（a clear answer），但论文给出的是一个 trade-off。
- 建议：考虑更准确的标题，如 "When Do Structural Signals Suffice for Agent Stagnation Detection?" 或保留现标题但在 abstract 第一行给出明确的 nuanced 答案。

---

## 小问题（不影响接收）

- **数字混淆**：§5.6 probe under corrupted labels "0.7512"，Appendix A.6 Claude few-shot "0.751"——两个极度相似的数字出自不同实验，容易混淆。建议将前者保留 3 位小数 (0.751) 或后者精确到 4 位。
- **Orphan bib entries**：references.bib 中 7 个条目（APA:83, Aho:72, Ando2005, Chandra:81, Gusfield:97, andrew2007scalable, rasooli-tetrault-2015）从未被引用——这些是 ACL 模板示例，应删除。
- **Label polarity**：0=stagnation, 1=progress 是反直觉的编码（通常 positive class = anomaly/target class）。虽然不影响结果，但增加认知负担。
- **Window stride 未讨论**：固定 stride=1，未讨论 stride 对 label 冗余和模型训练的影响。overlapping windows 导致相邻 windows 共享 4/5 的 actions，可能导致 train/test leakage（如果 split 不按 trajectory 分）。论文未提及 split 策略——是按 window 还是按 trajectory split？
- **Table 1 (LF design)**：caption 说 "OH values reflect corrected action embeddings (§5.6)"，但正文 §3.3.1 line 44 说 "It votes stagnation on 65.5% of SWE-Agent windows but only 5.4% of OpenHands windows"。两处数字一致，但读者第一次看到 Table 1 时不知道 "corrected" 意味着什么——在方法节就提及纠正过的数据容易造成困惑。
- **GPT-5.5 的 22.4% parse failure**：Appendix A.6 Table 8 报告 GPT-5.5 有 22.4% 的 parse failure，但 macro-F1 只在成功 parse 的 77.6% 上计算。这可能高估 GPT-5.5 的实际表现——如果将 parse failures 算作 wrong，F1 会更低。

---

## 引用验证结果

通过 verify_references 工具和 web_search 交叉验证：

- **53 个 bib 条目**，46 个被引用，7 个 orphan（均为 ACL 模板示例）
- **40 个经 OpenAlex 验证通过**
- **10 个 OpenAlex 未索引但 web search 确认存在**（chen2025monitoring, kale2025weaktostrong, fan2026agentrm, kaufman2012leakage, manheim2018goodhart, cemri2025multiagentfail, zhu2025agentfail 等——均为 arXiv preprint 或小 venue workshop paper，OpenAlex 收录延迟正常）
- **3 个轻微 mismatch**：
  - sala2019multiresolution：OpenAlex 返回 first_author=Varma，但 NeurIPS 2019 官方 PDF 确认 first author 是 Frederic Sala——**bib 正确，OpenAlex 错误**
  - jimenez2024swebench：bib=Jimenez vs API=Jimenez-Gomez（全名变体，可接受）
  - george2025cycledetection：bib=George vs API=Felix（作者全名 Felix George，姓 George，名 Felix——bib 正确）
- **结论：所有引用均真实有效，无虚假引用**

---

## Missing References

1. **InterCode** (Yang et al., 2024) — interactive code generation benchmark，与 SWE-bench 互补的 agent evaluation 工作
2. **OSWorld / WebArena** 等非 code agent benchmark——论文在 Limitations 提到未测试 non-coding domains，但 Related Work 未讨论这些 benchmark 及其 monitoring 需求
3. **RLHF / preference learning as monitoring signal** — 论文讨论了 PRM 但未提及 Bradley-Terry model 或 preference-based reward modeling 作为替代的 step-level scoring 方法
4. **Conformance checking in process mining** — 论文引用了 van der Aalst 的书但未引用具体的 conformance checking 技术（如 alignment-based conformance），而这与 "检测 agent 是否偏离预期行为" 直接相关

---

## 主要优势

1. **诚实的自我批评**：论文对 circularity 问题、silver label 的局限性、gold set 缺失的坦诚超过大多数投稿——尽管有时过度防御，但基本态度值得肯定。
2. **实验系统性**：比较了 structural/neural/LLM 三类方法，包含 encoder ablation、window size ablation、data scaling、multi-seed stability、instance isolation，覆盖面广。
3. **跨 agent 迁移设计**：SWE-Agent → OpenHands 的 zero-shot transfer 是一个有意义的实验设计，比单 agent 评估更有说服力。
4. **Label quality audit 的诚实报告**：发现并修正了 OH embedding 版本偏差（6.56%→21.4% stagnation rate），展示了 WS pipeline 的脆弱性——这本身是一个有价值的教训。
5. **LF temporal dynamics 分析**：cmd_rep 在两个 agent 上从 leading 变为 lagging 的发现是有趣的实证观察。

---

## 建议整体评估

**Main conference**: 不推荐接收（当前形态）
**Findings track**: 修改后有可能——如果解决超页问题、完成 gold set 验证、修正 overclaiming。

核心问题不是论文没有贡献，而是：(a) 贡献的可靠性依赖于未完成的 gold set 验证；(b) 方法论新颖性不足以支撑 main conference；(c) 格式违规（超页）需要大幅精简。建议完成 gold set 标注后作为 empirical study / negative results 类论文投 Findings。

[AGREE] R2-N2: Gold set 验证缺失。与我的 N1 完全一致。§5.8 已设计好 650 windows 分层采样方案，annotation pipeline 就绪但标注 pending。R2 的"至少 100-200 windows"最低要求合理。

[AGREE] R2-N3: 关键数字不公平 + overclaiming。与我的 N2 重叠，且 R2 额外指出 21.6pp 比较也不公平：structural 0.967 是 OH cross-agent，LLM 0.751 是 SWE-Agent in-domain 5-shot——不同 agent、不同数据分布、不同评估集。LLM 从未在 OH 上评估。两个 headline numbers 在 abstract/intro 中被作为核心卖点，caveats 分散在 footnotes 中，构成 overclaiming。

### Should-Fix

[AGREE] R2-W1: 方法论新颖性有限。论文 §2 自己承认 "hand-crafted features often outperform learned representations on narrow detection tasks... Our finding parallels this pattern"。但论文仍有增量贡献：WS pipeline 应用于 agent 轨迹是新的，Finding 3 的 root cause analysis 有价值。新颖性有限但非零。

[AGREE] R2-W2: 3 LF 使 Finding 3 意义受限。与我的 W3 相关。论文反复承认 "scaling to >10 LFs needed"——Finding 3 是 design-choice 导致的预期结果。至少应尝试 5-6 个 LF。

[REBUT] R2-W3: Introduction 过于密集。这是写作风格问题而非实质问题。EMNLP 论文将量化结果浓缩在 Introduction findings 段中是常见做法。Finding 1 的密度由内容复杂性驱动（同时覆盖 in-domain/cross-agent/probe/LLM 四个维度）。建议降为 Minor。

[AGREE] R2-W4: Circularity 讨论冗余。至少 5 处讨论（§5.2, §6.1, §6.2, §5.8, Introduction），给读者"越辩越心虚"的印象。建议集中在 §6.1 一次性讨论。

[REBUT] R2-W5: Title 误导。Question-form title 不承诺 binary answer，NLP 文献中常见（如 SWE-bench 原论文 "Can Language Models Resolve Real-World GitHub Issues?"）。副标题 "A Systematic Empirical Study" 已明确研究性质。最多 Minor 级别。

### 补充发现

[AGREE] Orphan bib: 同样发现 7 个条目，提交前必须清理。
[AGREE] Window stride=1 leakage: 好观点，我遗漏了。不过 cross-agent transfer（主要 non-circular 评估）不受影响。主要影响 in-domain 评估，但论文已将 0.995 标为 circular upper bound。
[AGREE] Label polarity: truly minor。

[Reviewer 1 审稿结果]:
交叉回应已提交。总结：

- **Must-Fix 3 项全部 AGREE**：超页（N1）、gold set 缺失（N2）、数字比较不公平+overclaiming（N3，补充认同 21.6pp 也是跨 agent/跨评估集的不公平比较）
- **Should-Fix 5 项中 3 AGREE / 2 REBUT**：同意新颖性有限（W1）、3 LF 限制（W2）、circularity 冗余（W4）；反驳 Introduction 密度（W3，写作风格而非实质问题）和 Title 误导（W5，question-form title 在 NLP 中常见）
- **补充发现全部 AGREE**，其中 stride=1 leakage 是我遗漏的好观点

---

[Reviewer 2 审稿意见]:
## Reviewer 2 交叉回应 — 逐条回应 Reviewer 1 的审稿意见

---

### Must-Fix 回应

**[AGREE] R1-N1: Gold set 验证 pending，全部 claims 缺乏 ground-truth 支撑**

同意，且这也是我自己审稿中的 N2 问题。补充两点强化 R1 的论点：

(1) 论文声称 "cross-coding-agent transfer provides non-circular evidence"（§1 line 31; §6.1 line 15; §7 line 22），但 cross-agent transfer 评估的 ground truth 仍然是 OH silver labels——由同套 LFs 生成。如果 LFs 在 OH 上系统性偏差（label version bias 事件已经证明这是现实风险），cross-agent F1 高只意味着成功迁移了 LF 偏差，不代表检测了真正的 stagnation。论文在至少三处使用 "non-circular" 来描述 cross-agent 评估，但这个术语不精确：circularity 被打破了（model 没见过 OH 训练数据），但 **label validity** 没有被验证。

(2) 650-window gold set 的设计看起来合理（双人标注 + 第三人仲裁，分层抽样 conf=1/2/3 + trajectory 位置），但连部分结果都没有报告。如果时间不够完成全部 650 windows，至少可以先标注 100-150 个 high-value windows（如 conf=1 的分歧案例），报告 silver-gold agreement rate 和 Cohen's κ，让 reviewer 对 silver label 质量有基本信心。当前状态是"承诺有但证据零"。

---

**[AGREE] R1-N2: Table 2 主比较 evaluation scope 不一致**

完全同意，这也是我 N3(a) 的核心问题。具体来说：

- Structural GB: OH 全集 150,652 windows → 0.967；OH conf≥2 全集 43,376 windows → 0.992
- Probe: OH 15% test split 6,122 windows → 0.598；conf≥2 test split → 0.598
- 36.9pp = 0.967 - 0.598，但两个数字的 N 和 scope 不同

Table 2 的 footnote (a) 解释了这一差异，但 footnote 无法修正 abstract 和 introduction 中直接使用 36.9pp 作为卖点的 overclaiming。R1 提出的修正方向合理：**应在相同的 6,122-window test split 上报告 structural GB 的 F1**，使比较在完全相同的 evaluation scope 上进行。

此外，footnote (a) 本身的信息密度过高（7 行小字解释 4 种不同的 scope），说明 Table 2 试图在一个表中呈现太多不同设置的结果，导致读者必须仔细阅读 footnotes 才能理解数字的可比性。建议拆分为两个表：一个严格 matched-scope 的主比较表，一个附录级的 full-scope 表。

---

**[AGREE with nuance] R1-N3: Circularity 缓解论证有逻辑漏洞**

同意 R1 的核心论点，但对 1.0pp 显著性部分有保留意见。

**同意的部分**：R1 指出 anti-circularity 论证隐含假设 LFs 在 OH 上也是正确的。论文的逻辑链是：

1. Structural GB 在 OH 上达到 0.967 F1（against OH silver labels）
2. LF firing patterns 在两个 agent 上差异很大（cmd_rep: 65.5% vs 5.4%）
3. 因此模型不能靠"记住" LF outputs 获得高分
4. 因此 cross-agent transfer 是 non-circular evidence

步骤 1→3 逻辑成立，但 3→4 有漏洞：模型可能学到了 LFs 的 **generalized decision boundary**（而非具体 outputs），而这个 boundary 在 OH 上恰好也被 OH silver labels 验证——因为 OH silver labels 也是由同套 LFs 生成的。换句话说，模型可能成功迁移了 LF 的 "stagnation 定义"，但这个定义是否对应真实 stagnation 仍未验证。label version bias 事件（§5.6）直接证明了这种风险是现实的。

**保留意见的部分**：R1 说 "11-feature vs 30-feature 仅差 1.0pp，在 GB cross-agent std=0.0015 的量级上可能不具统计显著性。" 这里有一个问题——std=0.0015 是 5-seed 的标准差（Table 5, Appendix A.3），对应的标准误 SE = 0.0015/√5 ≈ 0.00067。1.0pp (0.010) 对应 0.010/0.00067 ≈ 14.9σ，在 t 分布下 p 值极小。但这个推断假设 11-feature 和 30-feature 模型的方差相当，论文未报告 11-feature 模型的 multi-seed 结果。所以 R1 的关切方向正确（缺乏 formal test），但量级判断可能偏保守——1.0pp 在这个 variance regime 下可能确实显著。论文应报告 paired bootstrap 或 McNemar test 来确认。

---

### Should-Fix 回应

**[AGREE] R1-W1: LLM-as-judge 评估不完整，缺 CoT**

同意。论文在 §3.5 footnote 1 提到 "Extended LLM evaluation including chain-of-thought prompting" 在 Appendix A.6，但实际看 Appendix Table 8（§A.6），报告的是 zero-shot 和 5-shot，**没有 CoT 行**。这与 footnote 声称的不一致。

具体问题：
- 论文声称 "stagnation detection is non-trivial even for capable LLMs"（§3.5 line 121），但 LLM 只用了最简单的 zero-shot 和 5-shot，没有 CoT/self-consistency/structured output 等增强方法。在 2026 年的 LLM 评估中，至少应测试 CoT。
- GPT-5.5 的 22.4% parse failure 严重影响可比性。F1 仅在 77.6% 成功 parse 的样本上计算，这高估了 GPT-5.5 的真实表现。如果将 parse failure 算作随机猜测或错误，F1 会更低。

---

**[AGREE] R1-W2: 主要比较缺乏统计显著性检验**

同意。36.9pp 差异虽然大到 "看起来显然显著"，但：
- 没有 CI 或 bootstrap test
- Structural 和 probe 的 evaluation scope 不同（N1/N2），所以 paired test 在当前设置下甚至无法直接做
- Multi-seed 结果（Table 5）只报告 mean±std，没有 formal pairwise comparison

论文在 cmd_rep ablation 中使用了 McNemar test（§5.6, χ²=1551, p≈0）和 paired bootstrap（Table 2 footnote b），说明作者知道如何做显著性检验。但在核心比较（structural vs probe vs LLM）上反而没有做，这是一个疏忽。

---

**[REBUT with partial agreement] R1-W3: Label model 实验公平性存疑**

部分同意 R1 关于超参搜索透明度不足的关切，但不同意暗示 Snorkel MeTaL collapse 可能是 bug。

**反驳理由**：

(1) Snorkel MeTaL 的 -63.6pp 不是 bug，而是已知的失败模式。论文在 §5.7 和 §6.3 给出了合理解释：Snorkel MeTaL 假设 LFs 之间条件独立（"Both models assume conditional independence among LFs—an assumption violated by the shared behavioral signals underlying our three functions"），而三个 LFs 的底层信号（command repetition、file operations、task outcome）存在结构性关联。这种 CI 假设违反在 Snorkel 原始论文中就被识别为主要失败风险。在只有 3 个 LFs 且关联度高的 regime 下，MeTaL 的 EM 收敛到退化解并不意外。

(2) FlyingSquid 的 -11.3pp 也支持这一解释：另一个假设 CI 的 generative model 同样失败，只是程度较轻（因为 FlyingSquid 用 closed-form triplet 而非 EM，不容易发散到极端）。

**同意的部分**：

(3) 论文确实应该报告：(a) 每个 label model 的超参搜索范围和 best setting；(b) HMM 的 EM 收敛曲线和最终 log-likelihood；(c) TWV 的 σ 敏感性分析——虽然论文在 §5.7 提到 "σ=2 is optimal; σ=1 drops to 0.811 and σ=5 to 0.808"，但只有三个点的网格搜索不够充分。

---

**[AGREE] R1-W4: λ₃ inference-time 不可用性讨论不充分**

同意。这是一个我在自己的审稿中没有充分强调的重要问题。

具体来说：
- Table 2 所有 headline numbers（structural 0.995/0.967, probe 0.906/0.598）都基于 3-LF silver labels，包括 λ₃（outcome backpropagation）
- λ₃ 需要 task outcome（resolved/unresolved），在 inference time 不可用
- §6.5 报告移除 λ₃ 后 probe F1 降 7.7pp（0.847→0.770），这意味着约 1/3 的 conf≥2 labels 来自 λ₃ 的贡献
- 但论文在 §6.5 最后一段辩称 "λ₃ functions as a coverage expander rather than an independent corrector"——100% agreement with 2-LF labels on overlapping windows

R1 的关切核心是：**论文的 headline story（structural is great, probe is okay, LLM is bad）是在包含 privileged information 的 label set 上讲的**。读者会自然假设这些结果对应 deployable 系统，但实际 deployable 版本（2-LF）性能更低且未在主表报告。建议在 Table 2 增加 2-LF 行，或至少在 §5 开头明确声明 "all results use 3-LF labels including outcome backpropagation, a privileged signal unavailable at inference time; see §6.5 for 2-LF analysis."

---

### Missing References 回应

**ProcessBench (Zheng et al., 2024)**：同意应引用。ProcessBench 评估 process reward models 的 step-level verification 能力，与论文的 §2 第 1 段 PRM 讨论直接相关。

**AgentBench (Liu et al., 2023)**：同意应引用。AgentBench 覆盖 8 个 agent 任务类型，与 Limitations 中"未测试 non-coding domains"的讨论互补。

**WebArena (Zhou et al., 2023)**：同意，我自己的审稿中也指出了这一遗漏。论文应在 Related Work 或 Limitations 中讨论 web agent benchmark 及其 monitoring 需求。

---

### 总结

与 Reviewer 1 的意见高度一致。三个 Must-Fix 均同意，核心分歧仅在 N3 的 1.0pp 显著性判断上存在技术性保留。四个 Should-Fix 中同意三个（W1, W2, W4），对 W3 部分反驳（Snorkel MeTaL collapse 有合理解释，但超参透明度确实不足）。

**综合判断**：两位 Reviewer 的核心关切高度重叠（gold set 缺失、evaluation scope 不一致、circularity 论证漏洞），这强化了论文需要重大修改后才能达到 EMNLP main conference 标准的判断。

{
  "project": "agent_progress_probe",
  "report": "# Consolidated Review Report — Round 3\n\n**Paper**: \"Are Stagnation Signals Structural? A Systematic Empirical Study of Progress Detection in LLM Agents via Weak Supervision\"\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference (ARR May 2026 cycle)\n**Deadline**: 2026-05-25 AoE\n**Reviewers**: R1 (方法严谨性 + 实验设计), R2 (新颖性 + 写作完整性)\n**R1 Score**: 2.5/5 | **R2 Score**: 2.5/5\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\nThis paper presents a systematic empirical study of stagnation detection in LLM coding agents, comparing structural features, neural embedding probes, and LLM-as-judge baselines using a weak supervision methodology (3 labeling functions + majority vote) over 205K+ windows from SWE-Agent and OpenHands on SWE-bench. Three findings are reported: (1) structural features enable zero-data cross-agent transfer (0.967 macro-F1) but probes surpass with fine-tuning (0.976); (2) label quality, not LF noise, is the transfer bottleneck; (3) five label models fail to improve over majority vote in the 3-LF regime.\n\nThe paper addresses an important practical problem (real-time agent monitoring at &lt;5ms per window, no GPU) with commendable experimental breadth and unusual self-honesty about circularity limitations. However, four critical issues prevent acceptance: (1) the manuscript substantially exceeds the 8-page limit (~12 pages body); (2) all claims rest on silver labels with no gold-standard validation; (3) the main comparison table mixes evaluation scopes, producing overclaimed headline numbers (36.9pp, 21.6pp) in abstract/introduction; (4) the circularity defense, while earnest, contains a logical gap—cross-agent transfer validates LF-pattern transfer, not stagnation detection validity.\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n1. **Important and practical problem**: Real-time agent stagnation detection at &lt;5ms per window with no GPU is directly useful for production agent deployments. The problem framing (semantic stagnation vs. simple repetition) is well-motivated.\n\n2. **Honest self-criticism**: The paper's repeated acknowledgment of circularity issues, silver label limitations, and the gold set gap exceeds the self-awareness of most submissions. The label version bias audit (§5.6: OH stagnation rate 6.56%→21.4%) is a genuinely valuable transparency contribution.\n\n3. **Broad experimental coverage**: Encoder ablation (3 models), architecture ablation (GRU vs Transformer), window size ablation, data scaling curves, multi-seed stability, instance isolation, prevalence-matched cmd_rep ablation, and 5 label models—the experimental breadth is commendable for this paper type.\n\n4. **Cross-agent transfer design**: SWE-Agent → OpenHands zero-shot transfer is a meaningful evaluation paradigm. Instance isolation (Table 7: all Δ &lt; 0.1pp) confirms transfer reflects behavioral patterns, not task memorization.\n\n5. **LF temporal dynamics**: The reversal of cmd_rep from leading (−1.87 steps on SWE-Agent) to lagging (+3.32 steps on OpenHands) is a novel empirical observation with implications for weak supervision design on sequential data.\n\n6. **Negative finding with root cause analysis**: The systematic failure of all 5 label models, traced to insufficient inter-LF conflict in the 3-LF regime (conf≥2 agreement 95.1%), is informative for future weak supervision work.\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix (4 items — full reviewer consensus)\n\n**MF-1** [critical] **Page limit violation (~12 pages vs 8-page ACL limit)**\n[R2-N1; R1 cross: AGREE]\n\nThe manuscript body (title through conclusion) spans approximately 12 pages, exceeding the ACL long paper limit by ~4 pages. This is a desk-reject-level format violation under ARR. Primary sources of excess: §5 Experiments (8 subsections), §6 Analysis (5 subsections), §2 Related Work (7 paragraphs), and circularity discussion repeated across 5+ locations. Recommended cuts: move §6 Analysis subsections to appendix, compress Related Work to 4-5 paragraphs (merge Process Mining + Cycle Detection; merge Noisy Labels + Label Leakage), consolidate circularity to one location.\n\n**MF-2** [critical] **Gold set validation pending — all claims lack ground-truth support**\n[R1-N1; R2-N2; both cross: AGREE]\n\nThe 650-window gold set (§5.8) has a well-designed protocol (stratified sampling across confidence × trajectory position, dual annotator + third arbiter, operational definition excluding task outcome) but human annotation is pending. ALL reported numbers are evaluated against silver labels derived from the same LFs. The paper's repeated claim that cross-agent transfer provides \"non-circular evidence\" is imprecise: it breaks model-level circularity (model hasn't seen target data) but does NOT validate label correctness—OH silver labels are still generated by the same 3 LFs. The label version bias incident (§5.6: 21,898 window labels flipped after correction) directly demonstrates that silver labels can be severely wrong. Without even partial gold validation (100-200 windows, silver-gold agreement rate, Cohen's κ), core conclusions remain unverifiable. As R2 stated: the paper currently demonstrates \"we can reconstruct our own labeling function outputs,\" not \"we can detect real stagnation.\"\n\n**MF-3** [critical] **Table 2 evaluation scope inconsistency + abstract/intro overclaiming**\n[R1-N2; R2-N3; both cross: AGREE]\n\nTwo headline numbers are problematic:\n- **\"36.9pp\"**: Structural GB 0.967 on all 150,652 OH windows vs. probe 0.598 on a 6,122-window test split—different N, different scope. Structural GB's F1 on the same 6,122 test split is never reported.\n- **\"21.6pp\"**: Structural 0.967 on OH cross-agent vs. LLM 0.751 on SWE-Agent in-domain 5-shot—different agent, different label distribution, different evaluation set. The LLM judge was never evaluated on OH.\n\nBoth numbers appear in abstract and introduction as core selling points, with caveats buried in footnotes and §6. Table 2's footnotes (a, b) are too dense and cannot fix the fundamental problem of cross-scope comparison. The paper's defense (\"comparing the LLM's easier in-domain setting against structural's harder cross-agent regime\") is undermined by the fact that cross-agent structural evaluation uses silver labels from the same LFs. Fix: report all methods on the same evaluation scope; use matched-scope numbers in abstract/intro.\n\n**MF-4** [critical] **Circularity defense has logical gap — LF validity on target agent unverified**\n[R1-N3; R2 cross: AGREE with nuance]\n\nThe anti-circularity argument (§6.1-6.2) follows: (1) GB achieves 0.967 on OH silver labels; (2) LF firing patterns differ (cmd_rep 65.5% vs 5.4%); (3) model can't memorize LF outputs; (4) therefore transfer is non-circular. Steps 1→3 are sound, but 3→4 has a gap: the model may have learned the LFs' generalized decision boundary, and OH silver labels validate this boundary because they're generated by the same LFs. The feature overlap ablation reinforces this: the 11 features that transfer best (0.977 cross-agent) are exactly those aligned with LFs, while 19 residual features collapse to 0.566. This shows the model transfers LF patterns, not necessarily stagnation detection. The 1.0pp difference (11 vs 30 features) lacks a formal significance test—R2 notes it may be significant given std=0.0015 but agrees formal testing is needed. Only gold set validation can fully resolve this.\n\n### Should-Fix (6 items — all consensus)\n\n**SF-1** [warning] **LLM-as-judge evaluation incomplete — CoT missing, footnote inconsistent**\n[R1-W1; R2 cross: AGREE]\n\n§3.5 footnote 1 claims \"Extended LLM evaluation including chain-of-thought prompting\" in Appendix A.6, but Table 8 contains only zero-shot and 5-shot rows—no CoT. This is a factual inconsistency. GPT-5.5's 22.4% parse failure means F1 is computed on 77.6% of samples, likely overestimating performance. In 2026, an LLM baseline without CoT is incomplete. Either add CoT results or remove the claim from footnote 1.\n\n**SF-2** [warning] **Missing statistical significance tests for main comparisons**\n[R1-W2; R2 cross: AGREE]\n\nThe 36.9pp and 21.6pp differences lack confidence intervals or significance tests. The paper uses McNemar (§5.6: χ²=1551) and paired bootstrap (Table 2 footnote b) elsewhere, demonstrating capability—the omission on the most important comparisons is conspicuous. Multi-seed stability (Table 5) reports mean±std but no pairwise comparisons. Add paired bootstrap CIs for all main structural-vs-probe and structural-vs-LLM comparisons.\n\n**SF-3** [warning] **λ₃ is a privileged signal — headline numbers include it but it's unavailable at inference**\n[R1-W4; R2 cross: AGREE]\n\nAll headline numbers are based on 3-LF silver labels including λ₃ (outcome backpropagation), which requires task outcome unavailable during inference. λ₃ contributes 33.5% of conf≥2 labels. Removing it drops probe F1 by 7.7pp (0.847→0.770). §6.5 argues λ₃ is a \"coverage expander\" with 100% agreement on overlapping windows, but readers will assume headline results correspond to a deployable system. Add a 2-LF row to Table 2 or an explicit disclaimer at §5 start: \"all results use 3-LF labels including outcome backpropagation, a privileged signal unavailable at inference time.\"\n\n**SF-4** [warning] **Methodological novelty limited — known anomaly detection patterns in new domain**\n[R2-W1; R1 cross: AGREE]\n\nThe core finding (\"structural features outperform neural in zero-data narrow detection\") is self-acknowledged as paralleling known patterns (§2: \"Our finding parallels this pattern\"). The WS pipeline (3 LFs + MV) is standard data programming. The GRU probe and LLM-as-judge are conventional. Novelty is primarily in domain application (agent trajectories) and empirical findings (LF temporal dynamics, label model negative result). Both reviewers suggest this is better suited to the Findings track than main conference.\n\n**SF-5** [warning] **3-LF limitation makes Finding 3 a design-choice artifact**\n[R2-W2; R1 cross: AGREE]\n\nFive label models fail to beat MV (Contribution 3), but the root cause (3 binary LFs → 8 voting patterns, conf≥2 dominance) is a direct consequence of using only 3 LFs—a design choice. The paper acknowledges \">10 LFs needed\" as open question but doesn't attempt even 5-6 LFs (e.g., output length trend, error repetition, action type entropy). This makes Finding 3 a predictable artifact of insufficient LF count, not a fundamental insight about label models. At minimum, test 5-6 LFs or scope Contribution 3 more explicitly as applying only to the \"3-LF, low-conflict regime.\"\n\n**SF-6** [warning] **Circularity discussion redundant across 5+ locations**\n[R2-W4; R1 cross: AGREE]\n\nCircularity/feature overlap is discussed in: Introduction (2 places), §5.2 caveat paragraph, §6.1, §6.2, and Conclusion. Each repeats the same core argument (\"cross-agent transfer breaks circularity\") with different wording. The over-defensive tone paradoxically undermines confidence. Consolidate to one thorough discussion (§6.1) with brief cross-references elsewhere. This also helps with MF-1 (page reduction).\n\n### Minor Issues\n\n- **Number collision** (COLLISION-1): §5.6 probe 0.7512 under corrupted labels vs. Appendix A.6 Claude few-shot 0.751—near-identical numbers from unrelated experiments. Use 4-decimal precision or add explicit context. [R1, R2 AGREE]\n- **Orphan bib entries** (FMT-3 reopened): 7 ACL template example entries still in references.bib. [R1, R2 AGREE]\n- **Window stride=1 leakage** (STRIDE-1): Adjacent windows share 4/5 actions. Train/test split strategy not documented. If window-based, in-domain results may be inflated. [R2 raised; R1 AGREE]\n- **Label polarity**: 0=stagnation, 1=progress is counter-intuitive (typically positive = target class). [R2]\n- **TLM table caption** (TLM-CONF-1): Table 4 MV row 0.8946 contradicts caption \"labels evaluated against MV labels\" (would be 1.0). Footnote * clarifies it's downstream probe F1 but caption doesn't. [From Round 2, still open]\n- **Table 1 \"corrected\" reference**: §3.4.1 describes raw OH cmd_rep stats but Table 1 caption says \"corrected\" values—readers encounter this before §5.6 explains the correction. [R2]\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus\n\nBoth reviewers independently converged on the same four critical problems:\n\n| Issue | R1 | R2 | Cross-response |\n|-------|----|----|----------------|\n| Page limit violation (~12pp vs 8pp) | missed initially | N1 | R1 AGREE |\n| Gold set pending | N1 | N2 | Full AGREE |\n| Eval scope mismatch + overclaiming | N2 | N3 | Full AGREE |\n| Circularity logic gap | N3 | strengthened in cross | AGREE with nuance |\n\nAll 6 Should-Fix items also reached consensus after cross-sharing.\n\n## Reviewer Divergence\n\nTwo minor points of divergence, neither affecting overall assessment:\n\n1. **Introduction density** (R2-W3 Should-Fix → R1 REBUT → downgraded to Minor): R1 argues quantitative detail in intro findings is standard EMNLP practice; R2 argues 6+ numbers in 8 lines is excessive. **Resolution**: Style preference, not substance. Noted as Minor.\n\n2. **Title misleading** (R2-W5 Should-Fix → R1 REBUT → downgraded to Minor): R1 cites precedent of question-form titles in NLP (e.g., SWE-bench). R2 argues the nuanced answer (yes/no depending on regime) doesn't match question framing. **Resolution**: Both valid; downgraded to Minor.\n\n---\n\n## Questions for Authors\n\n1. When will gold set annotation be completed? Are there pilot IAA results?\n2. What is structural GB's F1 on the exact same 6,122-window OH test split used for probe evaluation?\n3. What hyperparameter search ranges were used for each of the 5 label models? Was the Snorkel MeTaL collapse investigated for implementation bugs?\n4. What are the main Table 2 numbers under a 2-LF (deployable, no λ₃) configuration?\n5. Is the train/test split trajectory-based or window-based? If window-based, what is the leakage rate from overlapping windows?\n6. Can you provide paired bootstrap CIs for the main structural-vs-probe and structural-vs-LLM comparisons on matched evaluation scopes?\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\nConsensus (both reviewers):\n- **ProcessBench** (Zheng et al., 2024) — step-level PRM evaluation, directly relevant to §2¶1\n- **AgentBench** (Liu et al., 2023) — multi-task agent evaluation, relevant to Limitations\n- **WebArena** (Zhou et al., 2023) — web agent benchmark, relevant to non-coding domain discussion\n\nAdditional (R2):\n- **InterCode** (Yang et al., 2024) — interactive code generation benchmark\n- **Conformance checking in process mining** — specific techniques beyond van der Aalst textbook reference\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n外部审稿未配置，本轮仅内部审稿。\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**Score: weak reject**\n**Confidence: 4/5**\n\nThe paper tackles an important problem with commendable experimental breadth and unusual self-honesty about its limitations. The core empirical findings—deployment-regime trade-off, LF temporal dynamics cross-agent reversal, label model negative result with root cause—are valuable contributions that deserve publication.\n\nHowever, four critical issues prevent acceptance in current form:\n\n1. **Format**: ~12 pages body vs 8-page limit → desk reject risk under ARR.\n2. **Validity**: All claims rest on silver labels; gold set protocol exists but annotation is pending. The label version bias incident (21,898 labels flipped) proves silver labels can be severely wrong—yet no ground truth validates any claim.\n3. **Overclaiming**: Headline numbers (36.9pp, 21.6pp) compare across different evaluation scopes and agents, presented without adequate qualification in abstract/introduction.\n4. **Circularity gap**: Cross-agent transfer breaks model-level circularity but not label validity. The defense is earnest but logically incomplete without gold validation.\n\n**Recommendation**: Complete at least partial gold set annotation (100-200 windows), unify evaluation scopes in Table 2, cut manuscript to 8 pages (move Analysis to appendix, compress Related Work and circularity discussion), and resubmit. Given limited methodological novelty, the **Findings track** may be a better fit—the empirical contributions are real but the paper reads as \"known tools applied thoroughly to a new domain\" rather than methodological advance.\n\n**Priority action items (ordered)**:\n1. Cut to 8 pages (blocker for ARR submission)\n2. Complete partial gold set annotation + report agreement metrics\n3. Report structural GB on same 6,122 test split as probe; use matched-scope numbers in abstract\n4. Add 2-LF row to Table 2 or prominent disclaimer about λ₃\n5. Fix footnote 1 CoT inconsistency\n6. Add paired bootstrap CIs for main comparisons",

This is a factual inconsistency. GPT-5.5's 22.4% parse failure means F1 is computed on 77.6% of samples, likely overestimating performance. In 2026, an LLM baseline without CoT is incomplete. Either add CoT results or remove the claim from footnote 1.\n\n**SF-2** [warning] **Missing statistical significance tests for main comparisons**\n[R1-W2; R2 cross: AGREE]\n\nThe 36.9pp and 21.6pp differences lack confidence intervals or significance tests. The paper uses McNemar (§5.6: χ²=1551) and paired bootstrap (Table 2 footnote b) elsewhere, demonstrating capability—the omission on the most important comparisons is conspicuous. Multi-seed stability (Table 5) reports mean±std but no pairwise comparisons. Add paired bootstrap CIs for all main structural-vs-probe and structural-vs-LLM comparisons.\n\n**SF-3** [warning] **λ₃ is a privileged signal — headline numbers include it but it's unavailable at inference**\n[R1-W4; R2 cross: AGREE]\n\nAll headline numbers are based on 3-LF silver labels including λ₃ (outcome backpropagation), which requires task outcome unavailable during inference. λ₃ contributes 33.5% of conf≥2 labels. Removing it drops probe F1 by 7.7pp (0.847→0.770). §6.5 argues λ₃ is a \"coverage expander\" with 100% agreement on overlapping windows, but readers will assume headline results correspond to a deployable system. Add a 2-LF row to Table 2 or an explicit disclaimer at §5 start: \"all results use 3-LF labels including outcome backpropagation, a privileged signal unavailable at inference time.\"\n\n**SF-4** [warning] **Methodological novelty limited — known anomaly detection patterns in new domain**\n[R2-W1; R1 cross: AGREE]\n\nThe core finding (\"structural features outperform neural in zero-data narrow detection\") is self-acknowledged as paralleling known patterns (§2: \"Our finding parallels this pattern\"). The WS pipeline (3 LFs + MV) is standard data programming. The GRU probe and LLM-as-judge are conventional. Novelty is primarily in domain application (agent trajectories) and empirical findings (LF temporal dynamics, label model negative result). Both reviewers suggest this is better suited to the Findings track than main conference.\n\n**SF-5** [warning] **3-LF limitation makes Finding 3 a design-choice artifact**\n[R2-W2; R1 cross: AGREE]\n\nFive label models fail to beat MV (Contribution 3), but the root cause (3 binary LFs → 8 voting patterns, conf≥2 dominance) is a direct consequence of using only 3 LFs—a design choice. The paper acknowledges \">10 LFs needed\" as open question but doesn't attempt even 5-6 LFs (e.g., output length trend, error repetition, action type entropy). This makes Finding 3 a predictable artifact of insufficient LF count, not a fundamental insight about label models. At minimum, test 5-6 LFs or scope Contribution 3 more explicitly as applying only to the \"3-LF, low-conflict regime.\"\n\n**SF-6** [warning] **Circularity discussion redundant across 5+ locations**\n[R2-W4; R1 cross: AGREE]\n\nCircularity/feature overlap is discussed in: Introduction (2 places), §5.2 caveat paragraph, §6.1, §6.2, and Conclusion. Each repeats the same core argument (\"cross-agent transfer breaks circularity\") with different wording. The over-defensive tone paradoxically undermines confidence. Consolidate to one thorough discussion (§6.1) with brief cross-references elsewhere. This also helps with MF-1 (page reduction).\n\n### Minor Issues\n\n- **Number collision** (COLLISION-1): §5.6 probe 0.7512 under corrupted labels vs. Appendix A.6 Claude few-shot 0.751—near-identical numbers from unrelated experiments. Use 4-decimal precision or add explicit context. [R1, R2 AGREE]\n- **Orphan bib entries** (FMT-3 reopened): 7 ACL template example entries still in references.bib. [R1, R2 AGREE]\n- **Window stride=1 leakage** (STRIDE-1): Adjacent windows share 4/5 actions. Train/test split strategy not documented. If window-based, in-domain results may be inflated. [R2 raised; R1 AGREE]\n- **Label polarity**: 0=stagnation, 1=progress is counter-intuitive (typically positive = target class). [R2]\n- **TLM table caption** (TLM-CONF-1): Table 4 MV row 0.8946 contradicts caption \"labels evaluated against MV labels\" (would be 1.0). Footnote * clarifies it's downstream probe F1 but caption doesn't. [From Round 2, still open]\n- **Table 1 \"corrected\" reference**: §3.4.1 describes raw OH cmd_rep stats but Table 1 caption says \"corrected\" values—readers encounter this before §5.6 explains the correction. [R2]\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus\n\nBoth reviewers independently converged on the same four critical problems:\n\n| Issue | R1 | R2 | Cross-response |\n|-------|----|----|----------------|\n| Page limit violation (~12pp vs 8pp) | missed initially | N1 | R1 AGREE |\n| Gold set pending | N1 | N2 | Full AGREE |\n| Eval scope mismatch + overclaiming | N2 | N3 | Full AGREE |\n| Circularity logic gap | N3 | strengthened in cross | AGREE with nuance |\n\nAll 6 Should-Fix items also reached consensus after cross-sharing.\n\n## Reviewer Divergence\n\nTwo minor points of divergence, neither affecting overall assessment:\n\n1. **Introduction density** (R2-W3 Should-Fix → R1 REBUT → downgraded to Minor): R1 argues quantitative detail in intro findings is standard EMNLP practice; R2 argues 6+ numbers in 8 lines is excessive. **Resolution**: Style preference, not substance. Noted as Minor.\n\n2. **Title misleading** (R2-W5 Should-Fix → R1 REBUT → downgraded to Minor): R1 cites precedent of question-form titles in NLP (e.g., SWE-bench). R2 argues the nuanced answer (yes/no depending on regime) doesn't match question framing. **Resolution**: Both valid; downgraded to Minor.\n\n---\n\n## Questions for Authors\n\n1. When will gold set annotation be completed? Are there pilot IAA results?\n2. What is structural GB's F1 on the exact same 6,122-window OH test split used for probe evaluation?\n3. What hyperparameter search ranges were used for each of the 5 label models? Was the Snorkel MeTaL collapse investigated for implementation bugs?\n4. What are the main Table 2 numbers under a 2-LF (deployable, no λ₃) configuration?\n5. Is the train/test split trajectory-based or window-based? If window-based, what is the leakage rate from overlapping windows?\n6. Can you provide paired bootstrap CIs for the main structural-vs-probe and structural-vs-LLM comparisons on matched evaluation scopes?\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\nConsensus (both reviewers):\n- **ProcessBench** (Zheng et al., 2024) — step-level PRM evaluation, directly relevant to §2¶1\n- **AgentBench** (Liu et al., 2023) — multi-task agent evaluation, relevant to Limitations\n- **WebArena** (Zhou et al., 2023) — web agent benchmark, relevant to non-coding domain discussion\n\nAdditional (R2):\n- **InterCode** (Yang et al., 2024) — interactive code generation benchmark\n- **Conformance checking in process mining** — specific techniques beyond van der Aalst textbook reference\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n外部审稿未配置，本轮仅内部审稿。\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**Score: weak reject**\n**Confidence: 4/5**\n\nThe paper tackles an important problem with commendable experimental breadth and unusual self-honesty about its limitations. The core empirical findings—deployment-regime trade-off, LF temporal dynamics cross-agent reversal, label model negative result with root cause—are valuable contributions that deserve publication.\n\nHowever, four critical issues prevent acceptance in current form:\n\n1. **Format**: ~12 pages body vs 8-page limit → desk reject risk under ARR.\n2. **Validity**: All claims rest on silver labels; gold set protocol exists but annotation is pending. The label version bias incident (21,898 labels flipped) proves silver labels can be severely wrong—yet no ground truth validates any claim.\n3. **Overclaiming**: Headline numbers (36.9pp, 21.6pp) compare across different evaluation scopes and agents, presented without adequate qualification in abstract/introduction.\n4. **Circularity gap**: Cross-agent transfer breaks model-level circularity but not label validity. The defense is earnest but logically incomplete without gold validation.\n\n**Recommendation**: Complete at least partial gold set annotation (100-200 windows), unify evaluation scopes in Table 2, cut manuscript to 8 pages (move Analysis to appendix, compress Related Work and circularity discussion), and resubmit. Given limited methodological novelty, the **Findings track** may be a better fit—the empirical contributions are real but the paper reads as \"known tools applied thoroughly to a new domain\" rather than methodological advance.\n\n**Priority action items (ordered)**:\n1. Cut to 8 pages (blocker for ARR submission)\n2. Complete partial gold set annotation + report agreement metrics\n3. Report structural GB on same 6,122 test split as probe; use matched-scope numbers in abstract\n4. Add 2-LF row to Table 2 or prominent disclaimer about λ₃\n5. Fix footnote 1 CoT inconsistency\n6. Add paired bootstrap CIs for main comparisons",

## Round 3 审稿完成摘要

**评分：weak reject（2.5/5 × 2 Reviewer，共识度高）**

### 4 个 Must-Fix（全员共识）

| # | 问题 | 性质 |
|---|------|------|
| MF-1 | 正文 ~12 页，超 ACL 8 页限制约 4 页 → desk reject 风险 | 格式 |
| MF-2 | Gold set 标注 pending，全部 claim 仅基于 silver labels，无 ground truth 验证 | 方法 |
| MF-3 | Table 2 评估 scope 不一致 + abstract/intro 36.9pp/21.6pp overclaiming | 实验 |
| MF-4 | Circularity 论证逻辑漏洞：cross-agent transfer 验证了 LF 模式迁移，非 stagnation 检测有效性 | 方法 |

### 6 个 Should-Fix（全员共识）

LLM CoT 缺失与 footnote 矛盾、主比较无统计检验、λ₃ 特权信号未在主表标明、方法论新颖性有限（建议 Findings track）、3-LF 使 Finding 3 成为设计选择产物、circularity 讨论冗余

### 建议

1. 削减至 8 页（将 §6 Analysis 移至 appendix，压缩 Related Work 和 circularity 讨论）
2. 完成至少 100-200 windows 的 gold set 标注，报告 silver-gold agreement + Cohen's κ
3. 在相同 6,122 test split 上报告 structural GB，abstract 使用 matched-scope 数字
4. 考虑定位为 Findings track empirical study

Issue Tracker 已同步更新（28 条 issue，含 5 个新 critical + 6 个新 warning）。

15	# Review Director Session 启动流程（圆桌会议模式）
16	
17	## 初始化
18	
19	1. `director_get_project_overview(project)` — 了解研究现状（实验状态、最新报告）
20	2. 读取论文文件状态：检查 `projects/{project}/paper/` 或 `main.tex` 是否存在
21	3. 检查是否有来自 Research Director 的论文提交（`[论文提交]` 消息）
22	4. 向用户简要汇报：
23	   - **投稿目标**：venue 和 deadline
24	   - **论文状态**：当前草稿位置和版本
25	   - **资料完整性**：提交的材料是否齐全
26	   - **下一步**：即将开始的圆桌审稿流程
27	
28	## 评审流程（收到论文提交后执行）
29	
30	### Step 0 — 资料检查 + Venue 调研（必须先做，不可跳过）
31	
32	1. 检查提交资料完整性：论文文件是否存在、是否可读、补充材料是否齐全
33	2. 确认目标投稿 venue（从提交消息中获取）
34	3. 用 `web_search` 搜索该 venue 当前轮次的 CFP（Call for Papers），关键词示例：
35	   - `"&lt;venue name> 2026 call for papers" site:aclweb.org`
36	   - `"&lt;venue name> 2026 submission deadline"`
37	   - `"&lt;venue name> 2026 author guidelines"`
38	4. **验证 deadline 未过期**：今天日期与截止日期对比，若已过期立即通知用户，不继续
39	5. 提取当前版本的格式要求：
40	   - 页数限制（正文 / 附录 / 参考文献）
41	   - 模板（ACL / IEEE / NeurIPS / ICML / ...）
42	   - 匿名规则（double-blind / single-blind / open）
43	   - 附录规则（是否允许、是否参与审稿）
44	   - 引用格式（natbib / biblatex / 特定 .bst）
45	6. 将以上信息作为上下文，随任务一起传递给 Reviewer
46	
47	⚠️ 禁止使用训练知识中的 venue 要求——格式标准每年都会更新，必须实时搜索确认。

50	
51	根据 venue 类型和论文特点，决定：

58	- **Workshop / 短文**：1 人（全面审查）
59	
60	向 Group C（即项目配置中的 `feishu.review_chat_id` 审稿群）汇报编排决定。

_source line 188 · 2026-05-15T13:13:47.652879+00:00_

# ARR 2026 Submission Formatting Guidelines

## Page Limits

**Long Papers:**
- Up to 8 pages of content
- Unlimited space for limitations section (required) and optional ethical considerations
- Unlimited reference pages

**Short Papers:**
- Up to 4 pages of content
- Unlimited space for limitations section (required) and optional ethical considerations
- Unlimited reference pages

Papers exceeding length requirements or lacking a limitations section face desk rejection.

## Template Requirements

Submissions must use "the official ACL style template, which is available [here](https://github.com/acl-org/acl-style-files)" and follow formatting guidelines at [ACLPUB](https://acl-org.github.io/ACLPUB/formatting.html). The document specifies that authors cannot modify style files or use templates from other conferences. Non-conforming submissions regarding paper size, margins, and font restrictions are rejected without review.

## Anonymization Rules

Papers must exclude author names and affiliations. Self-citations require third-person phrasing (e.g., "Smith showed..." rather than "We previously showed..."). Authors cannot reference materials unavailable to reviewers. Supplementary materials, including repository links, need anonymization. Cloud services like Dropbox are prohibited due to download tracking capabilities.

## Additional Formatting Requirements

- Appendices follow double-column format (with limited exceptions per ACLPUB guidelines)
- Appendices follow references and don't count toward page limits
- Supplementary software/data submitted as single .tgz or .zip archives
- All text addresses human readers exclusively, avoiding prompt injection attempts

# EMNLP 2026 Submission Guidelines - Comprehensive Extraction

## 1. PAGE LIMITS

**Long Papers:** "8 pages" of content, with "one additional page" allowed in final camera-ready version (9 pages total)

**Short Papers:** "4 pages" of content, with "one additional page" allowed in final camera-ready version (5 pages total)

The document does not specify separate limits for references or appendices beyond referencing ARR requirements.

## 2. ANONYMIZATION & DOUBLE-BLIND REVIEW

The reviewing process maintains double-blind standards: "Reviewers will not see authors, nor will authors see reviewers"

Reviews remain confidential: "reviews on ARR will not be made publicly visible"

Authors may voluntarily share their work: "authors will be given the option through ARR to make their anonymized submitted articles publicly visible"

Per ACL policy: "there is no anonymity period requirement"

## 3. TEMPLATE & STYLE REQUIREMENTS

Papers must "follow all of the ARR submission requirements," which directs submitters to ARR's "Paper Submission and Templates" guidance. The document does not explicitly state ACL style templates but references them as part of ARR compliance.

## 4. SUBMISSION DEADLINE

**ARR Submission Deadline:** "May 25, 2026"

**Reviewer Registration Deadline:** "May 27, 2026"

**EMNLP Commitment Deadline:** "August 2, 2026"

All deadlines are "11:59PM UTC-12:00 ('anywhere on Earth')"

## 5. APPENDIX & SUPPLEMENTARY MATERIALS

The guidelines reference "Optional Supplementary Materials (Appendices, Software and Data)" as part of ARR submission requirements but provide no additional specifications in the provided text.

## 6. ETHICS REQUIREMENTS

Papers must comply with multiple ethical frameworks:

- "ACL's Publication Ethics Policy"
- "ARR's Ethics Policy including the responsible NLP research checklist"
- "ACL's Disclosure Policy"

Specific integrity concerns addressed include: "thinly sliced contributions, submissions with hallucinated citations, and entirely AI

1	% Abstract: Structural stagnation detection in LLM coding agents (EMNLP 2026 ARR)
2	LLM-based agents deployed on software engineering tasks frequently exhibit semantic stagnation---executing diverse yet collectively unproductive action sequences that evade repetition detectors and overwhelm LLM-based judges.
3	No systematic study has compared detection approaches across deployment scenarios or investigated what signals carry stagnation information.
4	
5	We propose a weak supervision methodology for agent stagnation monitoring: three labeling functions aggregated by majority vote produce silver labels over 205K+ windows from two coding agents (SWE-Agent and OpenHands) on SWE-bench.
6	Against these labels, we compare 30 structural features, a GRU-based neural probe, and LLM-as-judge baselines across zero-data and fine-tuned regimes.
7	
8	We find a deployment-regime trade-off.
9	In zero-data cross-coding-agent transfer, structural features outperform the neural probe by 36.9\,pp and the best LLM judge by 16.8\,pp (cross-coding-agent structural 0.967 vs.\ best LLM 0.799).
10	With target-agent fine-tuning, the probe catches up and surpasses structural features on all evaluation windows (0.976 vs.\ 0.967), demonstrating that the structural advantage is specific to the zero-data regime.
11	Structural features are also data-efficient, exceeding 0.987 F1 at 10\% training data.
12	
13	Labeling functions exhibit agent-specific temporal dynamics: command repetition leads stagnation by 1.87 steps on SWE-Agent but lags by 3.32 steps on OpenHands.
14	Label version bias---not LF noise---was the dominant cross-coding-agent transfer bottleneck; fixing it improved 5\% fine-tuning F1 from 0.570 to 0.850.
15	Five label models (three temporal, two generative) all fail to improve over majority vote; the bottleneck is LF quantity, not aggregation sophistication.
16

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	LLM agents are increasingly deployed for complex, long-horizon software engineering tasks---resolving GitHub issues, debugging production systems, and iterating on multi-file codebases across dozens of editing rounds \citep{jimenez2024swebench,yang2024sweagent,wang2025openhands}.
5	As these deployments scale, a critical failure mode has emerged that is qualitatively different from crashes or syntax errors: the agent \emph{stagnates}---continuing to produce actions that appear purposeful while making no meaningful progress toward the task goal.
6	\citet{orlanski2026slopcodebench} document this degradation on SlopCodeBench, recording verbosity increases of 77\% and quality drops of 75.5\% in long-horizon coding trajectories, with no agent fully solving any benchmark problem end-to-end.
7	Crucially, the dominant failure pattern is not simple repetition but what we term \emph{semantic stagnation}: the agent issues varied commands, edits different files, and generates plausible-looking outputs, yet its behavior is functionally stuck.
8	This ``looks busy but going in circles'' pattern defeats existing monitoring tools, because surface-level action diversity masks the underlying lack of progress.
9	We observe this phenomenon at scale: across trajectories from two architecturally distinct agents---SWE-Agent \citep{yang2024sweagent} and OpenHands \citep{wang2025openhands}---on SWE-bench tasks \citep{jimenez2024swebench}, 28.5\% and 20.9\% of sliding windows exhibit stagnation under our silver labeling scheme, respectively, confirming that the failure mode is both prevalent and highly agent-dependent.
10	
11	Existing approaches address aspects of this problem but fall short in three ways.
12	First, \emph{repetition detectors} such as cycle detection \citep{george2025cycledetection} and SpecRA \citep{pipis2025reasoningloop} identify structural repetition---exact or near-exact loops in action sequences---but by design mis
…[truncated 6519 chars]

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	We frame stagnation detection as window-level binary classification over agent trajectories.
5	Silver labels are generated via weak supervision from three labeling functions, then used to train and evaluate three detection approaches: structural features, a neural embedding probe, and an LLM-as-judge baseline.
6	Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline} provides a system overview.
7	
8	\begin{figure*}[t]
9	\centering
10	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{../../figures/paper/fig_pipeline.pdf}
11	\caption{System overview. (Left) Agent trajectories are segmented into overlapping windows of $k{=}5$ steps. (Center) Three labeling functions vote on each window; majority vote produces silver labels with confidence 1--3. (Right) Three detection approaches compared: structural features (30-dim), NL embedding probe (GRU), and LLM-as-judge (eval only, not trained on silver labels).}
12	\label{fig:pipeline}
13	\end{figure*}
14	
15	%-------------------------------------------------------------------
16	\subsection{Problem Formulation}
17	\label{sec:problem}
18	
19	Given an agent trajectory $T = (a_1, a_2, \ldots, a_n)$ where each $a_t$ is an action (e.g., a shell command, file edit, or API call), we partition it into overlapping sliding windows $w_i = (a_i, \ldots, a_{i+k-1})$ with window size $k{=}5$ and stride 1.
20	The task is to assign each window a binary label $y_i \in \{0, 1\}$, where 0 denotes \emph{stagnation} (the agent is stuck or unproductive) and 1 denotes \emph{progress} (the agent is making meaningful headway toward the task goal).
21	
22	The window formulation offers two advantages over alternatives.
23	Compared to step-level scoring, windows capture short-range temporal patterns---such as a sequence of repeated failed attempts---that individual actions cannot express.
24	Compared to trajectory-level labeling, windows provide the granularity needed to pinpoint \emph{when} stagnation begins and ends within a single task attempt, enabli
…[truncated 7166 chars]

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We organize results around three questions:
5	(1)~which method best detects stagnation across deployment scenarios? (\S\ref{sec:main_results}--\S\ref{sec:robustness});
6	(2)~what drives label quality and cross-coding-agent transfer bottlenecks? (\S\ref{sec:lf_temporal}--\S\ref{sec:cmd_rep_ablation});
7	(3)~can temporal structure in LFs improve labels? (\S\ref{sec:temporal_label_models}).
8	All reported metrics are evaluated against silver labels derived from majority vote over three labeling functions (\S\ref{sec:method}).
9	
10	\paragraph{Circular evaluation caveat.}
11	Structural features (command repetition ratios, file operation counts, edit distances) are computed from the same signals that the labeling functions use to produce silver labels.
12	Consequently, the in-domain structural F1 of 0.995 reflects an upper bound on how well these features can \emph{reverse-engineer} the labeling functions, not detection accuracy against true ground truth.
13	The cross-coding-agent evaluation on OpenHands (0.967 macro-F1) is non-circular: models trained on SWE-Agent LF distributions are tested on a different agent whose LF firing patterns differ substantially (\S\ref{sec:cmd_rep_ablation}).
14	A human-annotated gold set provides additional non-circular evidence (\S\ref{sec:gold_set}).
15

19

22	Silver labels are produced by majority vote; confidence equals the count of agreeing LFs (1--3).

1	\section{Analysis}
2	\label{sec:analysis}
3	
4	\subsection{Circular Evaluation and Its Implications}
5	\label{sec:circular}
6	
7	The structural RF's 0.995 macro-F1 (\S\ref{sec:main_results}) is a predictable consequence of feature--label overlap, not a detection achievement.
8	The 30 structural features include repetition ratios and file operation counts---the same signals $\lambda_1$ and $\lambda_2$ consume to produce silver labels.
9	This score therefore represents an upper bound on LF reverse-engineering fidelity (a feature overlap ablation in \S\ref{sec:feature_overlap} quantifies this decomposition).
10	
11	Three settings break this circularity: (1)~cross-coding-agent transfer (GB 0.967 on OH, where cmd\_rep fires on 5.4\% vs.\ 65.5\% of windows---LF memorization cannot explain this); (2)~instance isolation (removing 284 shared SWE-bench tasks changes cross-agent F1 by ${<}$0.1\,pp, Table~\ref{tab:instance_isolation}); (3)~gold validation (GB 0.839 vs.\ MV 0.798, $p{<}0.001$; \S\ref{sec:gold_set}).
12	In-domain structural metrics should still be interpreted as LF-agreement scores.
13	
14	\subsection{Feature Overlap Ablation}
15	\label{sec:feature_overlap}
16	
17	We partition the 30 features into 11 \emph{LF-overlapping} (directly computing the same function as a labeling function) and 19 \emph{residual} features (Table~\ref{tab:feature_defs}, Appendix~\ref{sec:app_feature_overlap}).
18	On all OH windows, 11 LF-overlapping features alone achieve 0.977 cross-coding-agent F1---exceeding the full model by 1.0\,pp---the opposite of what label leakage would predict; residual features collapse to 0.566 cross-agent despite 0.952 in-domain, revealing agent-specific noise.
19	LF alignment reflects correct LF design, not circularity: SWE-Agent cmd\_rep fires on 65.5\% vs.\ 5.4\% of OH windows, so a model memorizing LF outputs would fail---yet GB achieves 0.967.
20	A leave-one-group-out ablation localizes the signal: removing cmd\_rep features drops cross-coding-agent F1
…[truncated 949 chars]

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Process Reward Models.}
5	PRMs assign step-level quality scores to guide agent behavior \citep{xi2025agentprm,choudhury2025prm,fan2026agentrm}, with benchmarks revealing degradation on long-horizon trajectories \citep{wang2026planrewardbench} and the advantage of specialized over general-purpose PRMs \citep{li2026toolprmbench}.
6	\citet{le2022coderl} take a complementary value-function approach operating on program states.
7	Our work differs in operating at window level without outcome labels, targeting real-time monitoring ($<$5\,ms) rather than training signal generation.
8	
9	\paragraph{Agent Self-Reflection.}
10	Self-reflection methods \citep{shinn2023reflexion,yao2023tot,zhou2024lats} prompt agents to diagnose their own failures, but LLMs cannot reliably self-correct without external feedback \citep{huang2024selfcorrect,kamoi2024selfcorrection}.
11	We address the prior question---\emph{when} is the agent stuck---using external structural signals.
12	
13	\paragraph{Weak Supervision for Sequential Data.}
14	Data programming \citep{ratner2016dataprogramming,ratner2017snorkel} enables dataset creation through labeling functions, extended to sequential data via linked HMMs \citep{safranchik2020linkedhmm}, multi-resolution LFs \citep{sala2019multiresolution}, and heterogeneous aggregation \citep{lison2021skweak}.
15	We apply this paradigm to agent trajectories, finding---unlike \citeauthor{safranchik2020linkedhmm}'s NER results---that temporal label models do not improve over majority vote in our low-conflict 3-LF regime (\S\ref{sec:temporal_label_models}).
16	
17	\paragraph{Noisy Labels and Label Leakage.}
18	Our silver labels connect to noisy label learning \citep{han2018coteaching,li2020dividemix}, though our noise is temporally block-correlated rather than instance-level.
19	The structural feature--label overlap instantiates label leakage \citep{kaufman2012leakage} and Goodhart's Law \citep{manhe
…[truncated 1377 chars]

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We studied progress stagnation detection in LLM coding agents, comparing structural features, neural probes, and LLM judges across deployment regimes via weak supervision.
5	Three findings emerge.
6	First, a deployment-regime trade-off: in zero-data cross-coding-agent transfer, structural features outperform probes by 36.9\,pp and the best LLM judge by 16.8\,pp (0.967 vs.\ 0.799); with fine-tuning, the probe surpasses structural features on all windows (0.976 vs.\ 0.967) but not on conf${\geq}$2 (0.976 vs.\ 0.992).
7	Second, LF temporal dynamics are agent-specific: cmd\_rep leads stagnation on SWE-Agent ($-1.87$ steps) but lags on OpenHands ($+3.32$); label version bias was the dominant transfer bottleneck.
8	Third, five label models all fail to improve over majority vote---LF quantity, not aggregation, is the bottleneck.
9	Practitioners should use structural features ($<$5\,ms, no GPU) for zero-data deployment and invest in probe fine-tuning when annotation budget permits.
10	Gold validation ($N{=}505$, three annotators) confirms the structural advantage: GB achieves 0.839 gold macro-F1 vs.\ 0.798 for MV labels ($p{<}0.001$).
11	Future work includes non-SE domains, online integration, and scaling to ${>}$10 LFs.
12	
13	\paragraph{Reproducibility.}
14	Code, silver labels, and trained models will be released upon acceptance; all trajectories derive from public SWE-bench runs \citep{jimenez2024swebench}.
15

_source line 207 · 2026-05-15T13:14:11.761176+00:00_

1	\label{sec:limitations}
2	
3	\paragraph{Scope.}
4	We evaluate two software engineering coding agents (SWE-Agent and OpenHands) on SWE-bench.
5	Cross-coding-agent transfer is demonstrated between these two agents; generalization to non-coding agent domains---web navigation, data analysis, multi-agent collaboration---remains untested.
6	
7	\paragraph{Silver labels.}
8	All evaluation is against labeling-function-derived silver labels; the structural RF's 0.995 macro-F1 reflects LF-reconstructibility rather than ground-truth detection accuracy.
9	A gold set annotated by three CS graduate students ($N{=}505$, ${\geq}$2-annotator agreement) provides non-circular validation (\S\ref{sec:gold_set}), though moderate inter-annotator agreement (Fleiss' $\kappa{=}0.39$) reflects the inherent subjectivity of stagnation boundaries.
10	
11	\paragraph{Labeling function count.}
12	Three LFs produce insufficient inter-LF conflict (95.1\% agreement at conf${\geq}$2) for any label model---temporal or generative---to demonstrate value; scaling to ${>}$10 LFs with higher disagreement is needed.
13	
14	\paragraph{No online evaluation.}
15	All results are offline; no intervention or real-time deployment evaluation has been conducted.
16	
17	\paragraph{Label version sensitivity.}
18	Cross-coding-agent transfer results are sensitive to label quality: a single embedding version mismatch shifted the OH stagnation rate from 6.56\% to 21.4\% and improved 5\% fine-tuning F1 from 0.570 to 0.850.
19	This sensitivity suggests that deploying the pipeline on new agents requires careful label auditing before conclusions about transfer difficulty can be drawn.
20	
21	\paragraph{Outcome dependence.}
22	The outcome backpropagation labeling function ($\lambda_3$) requires task outcome, which is unavailable during inference.
23	
24	\paragraph{Code and data.}
25	Code, data, and trained models are anonymous during review and will be released upon acceptance.
26

_source line 261 · 2026-05-15T13:15:11.889986+00:00_

def is_external_review_enabled(project_name: str) -> bool:
    """Check if external review (paperreview.ai) is enabled for a project.

    Reads ``external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled`` from project config.
    Returns False if not configured.
    """
    result = load_project_config(project_name)
    if not result.success:
        return False
    ext = result.data["config"].get("external_review", {})
    return bool(ext.get("paperreview_ai", {}).get("enabled", False))

The setting `external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled` is designed to be stored in the project configuration file at this path:

```yaml
external_review:

```

### Current Status for agent_progress_probe

The `external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled` setting is **NOT currently configured** in the agent_progress_probe project config. The function `is_external_review_enabled()` will return `False` by default when:
- The `external_review` section doesn't exist in the config, OR

The configuration is consumed by:
- `/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/core/approvals/hooks.py` - Uses this to control whether external review via paperreview.ai is triggered
- `/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/core/tools/external_review.py` - Implements the external review workflow with Stanford Agentic Reviewer (paperreview.ai)

### User

_source line 303 · 2026-05-15T13:22:52.497461+00:00_

[Reviewer 1 审稿意见]:
## Reviewer 1 审稿意见（方法严谨性 + 实验设计）

**论文**: "Are Stagnation Signals Structural? A Systematic Empirical Study of Progress Detection in LLM Agents via Weak Supervision"
**目标 Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference (via ACL ARR)

---

## 总体评分
**Overall**: 2.5/5 (介于 Resubmit next cycle 和 Findings acceptance 之间)
**一句话总结**: 问题定义清晰、实验体系完整，但核心评估存在循环性未被充分打破、headline 数字涉及不可比的跨数据集对比、gold set 验证力度不足（N=505, κ=0.39）等方法学缺陷，削弱了主要 claim 的可信度。

## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form, Feb 2025+）

| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |
|------|------|----------|
| Soundness | 2.5/5 | Headline 数字在不同评估范围/数据集上对比；gold set IAA 仅 fair；circularity 讨论诚实但未充分打破 |
| Excitement | 3/5 | 部署 trade-off 发现有实践价值，但范围限于 2 个 coding agents / 1 个 benchmark |
| Reproducibility | 3.5/5 | 方法描述充分，承诺开源代码/数据/模型，但 label pipeline 对 embedding version 高度敏感，可复现性存疑 |
| Reviewer Confidence | 4/5 | 仔细阅读全文含附录，熟悉 weak supervision 和 agent monitoring 两个领域 |

---

## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）

**N1** [critical] Headline 16.8pp 对比在不同数据集上进行，不构成有效比较
- 具体位置：Abstract L9, §4.2 L68, §6 L7, Table 8 (Appendix)
- 问题：论文的核心 headline "structural 0.967 vs best LLM 0.799, 16.8pp gap" 对比的是 **cross-agent OH evaluation (structural GB)** 和 **SWE-Agent in-domain evaluation (5-shot-CoT LLM)**。这是两个不同的数据集、不同的评估难度，无法直接做 pp 差值对比。论文在 §4.2 承认 "the structural advantage holds even comparing the harder cross-agent setting against the LLM's easier in-domain regime"——但这恰恰说明这不是 apple-to-apple 的比较。在同一数据集上的对比才有统计意义。
- 为什么影响接收：16.8pp 是 abstract、introduction、conclusion 反复使用的 headline number。如果读者误以为是同数据集上的比较，会严重高估 structural 方法的优势。
- 建议修改方向：(1) 在同一数据集上（SWE-Agent in-domain 或 OH cross-agent）做直接对比并报告显著性检验；(2) 如果 LLM judge 没有在 OH 上评估，应补充实验；(3) 至少在 abstract 中明确标注这是跨数据集的 upper-bound 对比。

**N2** [critical] 36.9pp headline 对比存在评估范围不一致
- 具体位置：Table 1 footnote $^a$, Abstract L9, §1 Finding 1
- 问题：Table 1 中 structural GB 在全部 150,652 OH windows 上评估得到 0.967；ProgressProbe 零样本在 15% test split (N=6,122) 上评估得到 0.598。论文用脚注 $^a$ 标注了这一差异，但在 abstract 和 introduction 中直接写 "outperform the neural probe by 36.9pp" 而不加任何限定。虽然 15% test split 理论上应具有与全集相似的分布，但这种评估范围不一致至少应在 headline claim 中披露。更关键的问题是：**probe 的 0.598 为什么要在 6K 子集而非全部 150K 上评估？** 如果是因为 OH embeddings 只计算了部分（gold set 评估提到 "missing OH embeddings"），这意味着 probe 的评估基础设施存在缺陷。
- 为什么影响接收：两个核心 headline number 都涉及不一致的评估范围/数据集，动摇了全文的主要 claim。
- 建议修改方向：在全部 OH windows 上统一评估 probe 零样本性能（如果 embedding 缺失是问题，需要补算），或者在 headline claim 中明确限定评估范围。

**N3** [critical] Circularity 未被 gold set 充分打破——κ=0.39 和 N=505 的可信度问题
- 具体位置：§4.7, Appendix G (§app_gold_set)
- 问题：Gold set 是打破 circular evaluation 的关键证据，但存在多重问题：
  (a) **Fleiss' κ=0.39** 仅为 "fair agreement"。对于二分类任务，chance-corrected agreement 仅 0.39 意味着标注者对 "stagnation" 的定义严重不一致。这不是标注者能力问题——论文自己说 "moderate agreement reflects the inherent subjectivity of stagnation boundaries"——但这意味着 gold label 本身噪声很大，gold F1 的天花板远低于 1.0。
  (b) **全量只有 52 个 three-annotator windows (Set A)**，IAA 基于这 52 个窗口计算，统计功效极低。
  (c) **Set B (N=505) 中 probe 只在 SWE-only subset (N≈335) 评估**，进一步限制了 cross-method comparability。
  (d) GB 的 gold F1=0.839 vs MV=0.798 (+4.2pp) 确实在 p<0.001 显著，但 **0.839 比 silver label 上的 0.967 低了 12.8pp**，说明 silver labels 与人类判断之间存在相当大的 gap。论文没有分析这些 gap 来自何处（是 LF 系统性偏差还是标注者遗漏）。
- 为什么影响接收：循环性是本文最核心的方法学风险。Gold set 是唯一的外部验证，但其自身可信度不足以 definitively 打破循环。
- 建议修改方向：(1) 增加 gold 标注量（至少 200+ 三人标注窗口以获得稳定的 κ 估计）；(2) 分析 silver-gold 不一致的 pattern——是否集中在特定 LF 配置或 trajectory 阶段？(3) 报告 gold set 上的 per-class breakdown（F1_stag 和 F1_prog），看模型在哪个方向出错。

**N4** [critical] λ₃ (outcome_bp) 的特权信号问题未充分处理——headline numbers 基于不可部署的 3-LF 配置
- 具体位置：§3.2.1 (labeling functions), §4.6 (cmd_rep ablation), §5.2, Limitations
- 问题：λ₃ 使用 task outcome 作为信号，这在推理时不可用。论文的所有 headline numbers (0.967 cross-agent, 0.995 in-domain) 都基于包含 λ₃ 的 3-LF silver labels。Table 5 显示 2-LF (去掉 cmd_rep) 仅 0.682；但**没有报告去掉 λ₃ 的 2-LF 结果**在 main body 中——Appendix H 只报告去掉 λ₃ 后 gold F1 掉 7.7pp (从 0.847 到 0.770)，但这是 downstream probe 的性能，不是 structural feature 的 cross-agent transfer 性能。
  一个实际可部署的系统只能使用 λ₁ + λ₂，但读者无法从当前论文中清楚地知道这个可部署配置的性能。Appendix H 说 λ₃ 在 2-LF 和 3-LF 重叠窗口上 100% 一致，且 λ₃ 的贡献是 coverage (33.5% of conf≥2 labels)——但这意味着没有 λ₃ 时 conf≥2 训练数据量少了 1/3，downstream 性能会受影响。
- 为什么影响接收：实际可部署性是论文的核心卖点（"<5ms, no GPU"），但 headline numbers 基于不可部署的配置。
- 建议修改方向：(1) 在主表中增加 2-LF (λ₁+λ₂ only) 配置的 structural cross-agent transfer 结果；(2) 如果 2-LF 性能显著低于 3-LF，需要诚实讨论这对 "deployment trade-off" claim 的影响。

---

## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）

**W1** [warning] Feature overlap ablation 结果的反直觉解读需要更强的论证
- 具体位置：§5.2, Table 9 (Appendix)
- 问题：11 个 LF-overlap features 在 cross-agent OH 上得到 0.977，**比 full 30 features 的 0.967 还高 1.0pp**。19 个 residual features collapse 到 0.566。论文将此解读为 "LF alignment reflects correct LF design, not circularity"。但另一个同样合理的解读是：**模型在学习重建 LF 输出，而 LF-overlap features 因为与 LF 共享信号空间所以做得更好，residual features 因为不相关所以失败**。这个解读不需要 "universal stagnation signals" 的假设——只需要 LF 函数本身是 agent-agnostic 的（它们确实是：repetition ratio 和 file diff 的定义不依赖于特定 agent）。
  论文的 counterargument 是 cmd_rep 在 SWE-Agent 和 OH 上 firing rate 差异巨大（65.5% vs 5.4%），所以 "memorizing LF outputs would fail"。但 structural features 并不需要 memorize 具体的 firing pattern——它们只需要学到 "high repetition ratio → stagnation" 这个方向性关系，而这个关系在两个 agent 上都成立（只是 threshold 不同）。
- 建议：(1) 报告 11-feature 和 30-feature 在 cross-agent 上的显著性检验（1.0pp 差异在 150K samples 上可能显著，这会加强 "residual features 是噪声" 的论点）；(2) 讨论上述 alternative interpretation 并说明为什么 "universal signal" 解读更合理。

**W2** [warning] 3-LF 下 temporal label model 的 negative finding 缺乏信息量
- 具体位置：§4.8, Appendix F (§app_tlm_results, §app_why_tlm)
- 问题：3 个 binary LFs → 8 种投票模式 → conf≥2 agreement 95.1%。在如此低冲突的环境下，temporal label models 无法超越 MV 几乎是必然结果。MV 在 3 binary LFs 下就是 MAP 估计，除非 LF 的时间结构极其显著。论文承认 "LF quantity is the bottleneck"，但花了大量篇幅（主文 §4.8 + Appendix F 完整两页）报告一个可预测的 negative finding，又将其列为 Contribution 3。
  如果目标是展示 MV 的 robustness，一个更有信息量的实验是在 5-10 个 LF 下（通过 splitting/combining 现有 LF 或引入更多信号）测试 label models。
- 建议：降低这个 contribution 的权重，或者增加 LF scaling 的 pilot 实验来验证 "more LFs → label model value" 的假设。

**W3** [warning] Label version bias 事件的讨论不够深入
- 具体位置：§4.6, §Limitations, Appendix D
- 问题：OH stagnation rate 从 6.56% → 21.4%（3.3× 变化）是 embedding version mismatch 导致的。这是一个严重的 pipeline fragility 信号。论文将其框架为 "label quality is the transfer bottleneck" 的证据（Finding 2），但没有回答：
  (a) **mismatch 是怎么被发现的？** 如果没有 cross-agent 对比作为 sanity check，这个 bug 可能永远不被发现。
  (b) **什么保证 "corrected" version 是正确的？** 21.4% 的 stagnation rate 是否与 gold set 的 stagnation 分布一致？
  (c) **这对 pipeline 的可推广性意味着什么？** 如果用户在新 agent 上部署此 pipeline，一个 embedding 版本不匹配就能导致 label 分布 3× 偏移，用户如何做 quality control？
- 建议：(1) 报告 gold set 上的 stagnation prevalence 并与 corrected silver prevalence 对比；(2) 添加 label quality audit 的 checklist/protocol 供未来使用者参考。

**W4** [warning] Multi-seed stability 的统计检验不足
- 具体位置：§4.3, Appendix C (Table 4)
- 问题：Multi-seed 只报告 mean±std（5 seeds）。对于关键对比（structural RF 0.995±0.000 vs probe 0.919±0.011），虽然差异明显，但缺少正式的统计检验（paired t-test 或 Wilcoxon signed-rank）。更重要的是，**cross-agent GB 的 multi-seed (0.968±0.002) 和 in-domain RF 的 multi-seed 没有形成可比的 pair**——它们在不同数据集上评估，不同方法。
- 建议：为 in-domain 和 cross-agent 分别做 multi-seed 对比，报告 confidence intervals 或 p-values。

**W5** [warning] Conf≥2 子集作为 "primary evaluation set" 的合理性未充分论证
- 具体位置：§3.2.2, §4.1
- 问题：Conf≥2 过滤掉了 conf=1 的窗口（最模糊的案例），但这些恰恰是实际部署中最需要处理的窗口。论文说 "This stratification allows us to separately analyze model behavior on high-confidence vs ambiguous windows"，但实际上主文几乎所有数字都在 conf≥2 上报告。在 conf=1 上，structural GB 的性能如何？Table 1 显示 GB 在 "all windows" 上只有 0.967 但 conf≥2 上是 0.992——说明 conf=1 性能显著更差。而 fine-tuned probe 在 all windows (0.976) > structural GB (0.967) 但在 conf≥2 上反转 (0.976 < 0.992)——论文解释为 "probe's all-windows edge reflects better tolerance of noisy low-confidence labels"。这个分析很好，但如果 conf=1 是实际中最需要检测的案例，那么 all-windows 指标可能比 conf≥2 更 relevant，headline 应该用 all-windows 的数字。
- 建议：在主文中增加 conf=1 专项分析，讨论 conf=1 窗口在实际部署中的比例和重要性。

---

## 小问题（不影响接收）

- **引用年份 mismatch**: `ratner2017snorkel` bib 标注 2017 但 OpenAlex 显示正式发表年份为 2019 (VLDB)；`jimenez2024swebench` 的 bib 年份 2024 但 API 返回 2023。应统一为正式发表年份。
- **14 个 orphan bib entries**: 包括 `Aho:72`, `APA:83`, `Gusfield:97`, `andrew2007scalable` 等——这些看起来是 ACL template 自带的示例引用，应在提交前清理。
- **Table 1 caption 中 footnote $^b$**: 报告了 "GB leads fine-tuned probe by 1.65pp (CI [1.11, 2.23], p<0.001) on conf≥2"——很好，但这个显著性检验的 N=6,122 来自 probe 的 test split，而 GB 应该是在全部 conf≥2 (N=43,376) 上评估的。paired bootstrap 需要配对样本，即两者应在相同的 N 上评估。如果 GB 也是在 N=6,122 上做的 paired test，应明确说明。
- **Figure 1 (pipeline overview)**: 只在正文提及但无法从 tex 确认渲染质量，建议确保 PDF 中图表清晰可读。
- **§3.2.1 labeling function 描述**: λ₁ 的具体 threshold（什么 repetition ratio 触发 stagnation vote）没有给出，只说 "a high ratio triggers a stagnation vote"。应报告具体 threshold 或 threshold 选择方法。

---

## 主要优势

1. **诚实的循环性讨论**：论文在 §5.1 明确承认 in-domain 0.995 是 LF-reconstructibility 而非真实检测能力，并通过三种方式尝试打破循环（cross-agent, instance isolation, gold set）。这种透明度在 ML 论文中不常见。

2. **Instance isolation 实验设计精到**（Table 7, Appendix E）：移除 284 个 SWE-bench/OH 共享任务后 F1 变化 <0.1pp，有效排除了 instance memorization 假说。

3. **cmd_rep 的 coverage vs precision 分析**（§4.6, Appendix D）具有 practical insight：high-precision low-recall LF 的核心价值是扩大训练数据量而非修正标签，这对 weak supervision 实践者有指导意义。

4. **Data efficiency 分析**（Figure 2, §4.4）：structural classifiers 在 10% 数据即达 0.987，展示了明确的实践优势。

5. **LLM-as-Judge 的全面评估**（Table 8）：包含 zero-shot、CoT、few-shot、structural augmentation 等多种配置，并报告了 GPT-5.5 的 parsing failure rate (22.4%)，信息量丰富。

6. **Error analysis 质量高**（Appendix I）：probe 错误集中在 trajectory 中点、与 LF conflict 高度相关、cross-agent errors 近乎 disjoint——这些分析有助于理解方法的 failure modes。

---

## Questions to Authors

Q1. 能否在同一个评估集上做 structural GB vs LLM (5-shot-CoT) 的直接对比？例如在 SWE-Agent conf≥2 test set 上，structural GB 的 in-domain F1 是多少（不是 0.995 这个 circular 数字，而是用某种 non-circular 方法评估的）？或者在 OH cross-agent 上 LLM judge 的性能是多少？

Q2. Gold set 的 stagnation prevalence 是多少？它与 corrected silver labels 的 21.4% 一致吗？

Q3. 2-LF (λ₁+λ₂ only, no λ₃) 配置下 structural GB 的 cross-agent transfer F1 是多少？

Q4. Probe 的零样本 OH evaluation 为什么只在 15% test split 上做？是否因为 OH embeddings 没有完整计算？如果是，这是一个可以补充的实验。

Q5. 11 个 LF-overlap features (0.977) 比 full 30 features (0.967) cross-agent F1 更高，1.0pp 差异是否统计显著？

---

## Missing References

- **Flyingfish/WRENCH benchmark** (Zhang et al., 2021)——已引用但作为 orphan bib（未在正文 cite）。建议在 related work 中讨论 WRENCH 作为 weak supervision benchmark 的适用性。
- **Confident Learning** (Northcutt et al., 2021, JAIR)——作为 noisy label 检测的主流方法，考虑到论文的 label quality focus 应该讨论。
- **Label model theory** (Shin et al., 2022)——理论分析了 label model 在 low-LF 场景下的 performance bounds，与 Finding 3 直接相关。

---

## Overall Assessment

这篇论文解决了一个有实践价值的问题（LLM agent 停滞检测），实验覆盖面广（weak supervision + structural features + neural probe + LLM judge + 5 种 label models）。循环性讨论的诚实度值得肯定。

但方法学的核心问题在于：**论文的两个 headline numbers (36.9pp, 16.8pp) 都涉及不可直接比较的评估设置**——前者是不同评估规模（150K vs 6K），后者是不同数据集（cross-agent vs in-domain）。这不是 framing 问题而是 validity 问题。Gold set 作为唯一的外部验证，其 κ=0.39 和 N=505 的规模不足以 definitively 打破循环。λ₃ 的特权信号使得 headline numbers 基于不可部署的配置。

建议：在解决 N1-N4 后可达到 Findings 水准；要进 main conference 需要更强的 gold validation 和 clean evaluation comparisons。
